- Added `chrome.border` (default `rounded`, joined heavy focused corners; `square` available) and `chrome.glyphs` (default `auto`, Unicode unless a real terminal reports a non-UTF-8 locale or `TERM=linux`). Opt out with `chrome.border=classic` and `chrome.glyphs=unicode`. `ascii` converts complete TUI frames at unchanged cell widths; `nerd` is explicit Nerd Font Mono opt-in. Added `Styles.Border`, `Styles.Glyph`, application `Appearance.SetGlyph` roles, and unbound `glyphs list` / `glyphs show` previews. No keys, modes, focus behavior or timers change.
- Added: match marks in palette completions, choosers, picker paths and inline suggestions through `chrome.<level>.match` and `palette.*` tone rows. Rich uses the mode colour, subtle the accent; selected matches keep bold inside the reverse bar. `chrome.color=off` preserves the previous output. Matching, keys and CLI completion JSON are unchanged.
- Fixed (F2): record-editor noun references show labels (id fallback) and always use searchable choices, even with `Free`. Partial queries never write; choosing writes the id, `(none)` clears optional associations deliberately, and undo restores them. Generated prop editors share the same behavior; free Enum/Source-only fields are unchanged.
- Fixed (F8): an empty `row:<field>` source for `target` refuses before a verb, confirmation or argument form can run, naming the field and row. Local and catalog-mounted rows share the check; empty sources for other parameters stay omitted.
- Added (F1/F10): `NounBuilder.EditKey(false)` / `Noun.NoEditKey` (`noEditKey` in catalogs) declines only a noun's implicit `i` edit fallback. Default on; `EditKey(true)` restores it. Explicit key rows, palette, CLI/API and the noun's edit availability remain unchanged. No default binding file changes.
- Fixed (F9): removing a modal's return instance now honors its surviving `FocusOnClose` target, just like a plain close. The modal keeps focus while open; closing it lands on the named target with normal focus/mode rules. Missing targets retain the ancestor fallback.
- Fixed (P12-F1): modal rows restore the panel foreground after full and foreground SGR resets, so match marks and other styled spans keep one continuous selected bar and unselected text keeps the panel ink. Explicit span colours remain intact; `chrome.color=off` preserves the previous bytes.
- Fixed (P12-F2): a `chrome.glyphs` observer refreshes live trees when the setting changes, including help trees drawn through their backing list. Preset and live glyph changes now agree without waiting for `Tree.Block`.
- Fixed (P12-F3): ASCII frames map the framework's en/em dashes, bullet/white bullet, timeline step bar, multiplication sign and return arrow to same-width ASCII marks instead of `?`. Paged-list ranges remain readable under non-UTF-8 locales.

## v0.88.0 (2026-11-04)

- Fixed (F13, F16): explicit-target edit titles name the edited subject, not a different focused row; confirm forms compare against the latest loaded store baseline.
- Fixed (F14): cancelling a write runs the general job-abandon hook, releases the thing's queue, preserves unsaved values in notices, and lets later edits write.
- Fixed (F15): undo barriers apply only to actual inverse-less proxy writes, not navigation or application verbs wrapping recorded changes. Cancelling a pending edit open is an action.
- Added: `quit.force-after` (duration, default `500ms`) ignores habit double-taps while writes drain; `0s` restores any-second-press force. Explicit `app.quit --force` remains immediate.
- Fixed (F5): leader fall-through shares the standard subject ambiguity check and refuses to guess between associated things.
- Fixed (F7–F9, F11): implicit forms name the edited thing, preserve whole-number text, clear optional typed props through set, and keep writing by key after a row disappears.
- Fixed (F4, F6): reopening implicit edit flushes and retargets its form; pending opens replace older reads, Escape cancels them, and late reads do not steal a changed interaction.
- Fixed (F3): forms serialize writes by stored thing across close/reopen, and reopened forms include pending values. `RecordSpec.WriteKey` shares that queue across editors without blocking unrelated records.
- Fixed (F10): implicit forms watch their noun and reload current values through get, honoring `forms.outside-write`. Record editors support asynchronous `Reload` and keyed `WatchArgs`; proxy nouns use existing watch invalidations without blocking the loop.
- Fixed (F2 safety): history records inverse-less writes as barriers and explains refusal instead of undoing an older thing. Implicit form opening is an action; its set requests remain changes. Mounted inverse execution needs a general asynchronous history inverse and is not implemented by a tool-specific branch.
- Fixed (F12): a write failing after the form closes preserves the thing, field and attempted value in a persistent notice and Messages, with secret values redacted.
- Fixed (F1): quit names pending writes and waits at most `quit.write-wait` (duration, default `10s`). A deliberate second `ctrl+c`/`q` after `quit.force-after`, or `app.quit --force`, exits immediately; other keys cannot open modals while draining. Unfinished thing/field/value records use persistent notices and stderr outside the alternate screen.
- Fixed (F3 completion): explicitly submitted forms with a `WriteKey` use the same per-thing write queue as change-mode forms, including pending-value overlays and bounded quit draining.
- Fixed (F9 storage): literal null clears an optional prop instead of using the boolean-toggle sentinel; required null is refused before a store write. Added store-level coverage for enum/boolean clearing alongside real boolean toggles.
- Fixed mounted catalog rows' `row:<field>` binding sources: fields fall back to the row's props while `row` keeps its opaque id. Catalog proxies without a `target` parameter accept `from.target` as foreign `--target` transport metadata; declared target parameters keep their meaning.
- Added host-only catalog `peerBindings` (`cli.PeerBindings`), a reversible soft peer-row layer (`Runtime.ReloadPeerBindings`, `PeerBindings`), missing-peer diagnostics in `bindings check`, and mounted extension versions. User bindings override/unbind the layer. `bindings.peer-notices` defaults to `user`; `all` also posts catalog warnings, `off` keeps both check-only.
- Added `cli.HostFromEnv`, `Host.Peers`, `Run` and explicit-boundary `Call`: callbacks preserve host global arguments, never execute a peer directly, never auto-confirm, and enforce a bounded call chain. The shell narrows callbacks to extension agents with `Actor.Via` provenance and persists/flushes their history. A protected host setting may explicitly opt into already-detected caller rights.

- Added: declared focus associations (`PropSpec.Association`, `Operation.ByAssociation`, `NounBuilder.ByAssociation`). Human targetless requests can act on one live thing referenced by the focus's Data; optional `Focus` parameters share the walk. Required parameters are never filled; CLI/API/MCP and other program requests still need explicit targets. `subject.from-association` defaults on; off preserves the old focus-path behavior. Ambiguous associations refuse with candidate names. The palette names `acts on: LABEL`, completed non-modal actions post a notice, and `association.list {noun}` exposes the live references without reading stores. Catalogs publish and mounts preserve the declarations.
- Changed: unbound leader keys now run eligible single-key outside bindings on the same page, through a general `ModeSpec.Outside` / `ModeOutside` mechanism. Declared associations are tried before the focus's own outside stack; existing leader rows still win. `leader.fall-through` defaults to `no-motions`, excluding motions and component-bound selection/editing (`Operation.FocusBound`, `.FocusBound()`). `all` includes them; `off` restores the previous forwarding behavior. Keys with outside rows no longer reach a nested program: use `ctrl+a ctrl+a` to send the leader, or switch fall-through off. Outside multi-key sequences and expanded leader help remain deferred.

- Added: standard `edit` for nouns with readable stores and writable declared props. `forms.edit-apply` defaults to `change`: text is debounced and flushed on field leave, close and quit; choices write at once; Escape keeps writes and undo replaces discard. `confirm` retains Enter-to-apply and Escape-to-cancel. Supplied props still write directly through standard `set`. Application editors keep precedence; `forms.edit` opts out before store or catalog registration.
- Added: normal-mode `i` as an app-level fallback for implicit editors, outside inputs and modals. Inner page, outline and input bindings keep their meanings. Mounted standard editors run locally using catalog props and the existing `get`/`set` proxies.
- Fixed: record-form choice fields load pending sources without blocking form opening or typing.
- Fixed: pending form saves are tracked through their existing job tickets, serialized, retained after close and drained before quit. Invalid or refused fields do not block other field writes. Commands scheduled while components inspect an event are returned in that same event's command batch.

## v0.87.0 (2026-11-04)

- Fixed: a request a program issues (an app's own `Execute` from a background poll, a hook, the CLI, MCP `run`) while a list is in visual mode no longer turns into `list.apply` over the selection. Only requests a person made from the UI (a key, a leader fall-through, the palette, a macro replay, and the form/confirm steps that keep their key's source) act on every selected row; a program's acts on its own target and leaves the mode and the selection alone. Before, a background `Execute` of a row verb ended V within a second and ran on every selected row ("done on N rows", or "list.apply is not applicable now" on a group header).
- Fixed: `list.hide-archived` now also applies to row-fed lists, trees and pages (rows with a `RoleArchive` prop true are hidden by default), not only to the standard `list` verb. A prop filter naming the archive prop (the saved `archived` filter) shows them, clearing the filter hides them again, and turning the setting off shows everything at once. `Runtime.archiveHiding` is the one predicate `runList` and the lists share. In a tree an archived node hides its whole subtree (a child cannot stand without its parent); the `archived` filter shows archived nodes with their ancestors as context. `List.Len`, the page title, the filter count and the empty line count only the rows the list is about, so a list whose rows are all archived shows its empty state; a page title shows `shown/total` whenever the filter hides rows. The app-side hide a consumer did on its own (o-lane) can go. Setting: `list.hide-archived`, default true, is the opt-out.
- Fixed: a list with blank lines above rows (`tree.space-above`) no longer scrolls past its end: `ctrl+f` or `G` at the bottom fills the window instead of leaving one row and empty lines, a taller terminal does not leave blank space below, and the selected row keeps the scrolloff margin again. Story `tree/gaps-ctrl-f-fills-window`.
- Added: an outline row left with no text is removed (setting `tree.remove-empty-rows`, ochazuke > Lists, default on): a new row left blank (`o`, then `esc`), or a row edited down to nothing. The blank text is never written; the removal is one undo step (a new row never filled in leaves none: its add is taken back), and the cursor lands on the row `o`/`O`/`a` ran on, else the next row, else the previous, else the tree's list, never on nothing. A row with children, a fixed row, a row whose delete the application refuses (`Tree.Refuse`) and a row that was already blank before anyone touched it stay. `enter` on a blank line replaces it with the next line instead of piling blanks up. Rows an `i`/`esc` of which changed nothing are not swept at load. A node can opt out by data: `TreeNode.Fixed` holding `TreeRemoveEmpty` (`remove-empty`) keeps a row that held text when its line is emptied (old text restored, nothing written, no undo step, a short notice); a row just added and left blank is still removed. Story `tree/empty-remove-empty-declared-stays`. Stories `tree/empty-*`; tests `TestTreeBlankNewRowIsRemoved` and friends. Consumers: matomeru's outline picks it up on the next tag (an emptied project row with no tasks is deleted, as `d d` would). A new row stays new across a row key (`tab`, `shift+tab`): `o tab esc` leaves no blank row however it was indented. Story `tree/empty-new-row-tab-esc`.
- Added: a tree can put a blank line above a row. `TreeNode.SpaceAbove` (JSON `spaceAbove`) and `ListItem.SpaceAbove` ask for it; one mechanism in all three row layouts (plain list, table, tall table), driven by the node's data, with no knowledge of what the row is. Never above the first row shown (a filter that makes a spaced row first, or a zoom, draws none), and never above the first row of a scrolled window. The blank line is not a row: `j`/`k`, `}`/`{`, marks, a visual selection and the row counts ignore it; the window, `x–y / n` and `ScrollLines` fit the lines it takes, the open line sits on its own row, and `List.Height`/`Tree.Height` count the gaps. Setting `tree.space-above` (ochazuke > Lists, default on) draws the rows with no gaps. Stories `tree/space-above*`; tests `TestTreeSpaceAbove*`. Consumers: matomeru's outline marks its project rows.
- Changed: deleting an outline row with children no longer always takes them along. Setting `tree.delete-children` (ochazuke > Lists): `promote` (default) deletes a collapsed row's whole subtree but, for an expanded row, only the row, its children moving up one level into its place, in order; `orphan` moves them to the top level instead, in order, after the row's top-level ancestor (in its place when the row is itself top-level); `subtree` is the old behaviour. The writes are the moves first, then the delete of the now-leaf row (a store writing each change never cascades or orphans a child), and undo is one step that restores the exact structure and order. Expanded means not folded in the view being drawn (a prop filter's own folds count while it shows context); hidden children of a folded row go with it. A fixed child no longer stops its expanded parent being deleted (it moves up); a fixed row still refuses. Over a visual selection `d` (new row `ochazuke.outline/visual-line d` → `outline.delete`) deletes every selected row as one undo step; a selected parent and child are each deleted once. The cursor lands on the first promoted child, else the next row, else the previous one, else the tree's list. The old name `Tree.remove` is internal; the verb `outline.delete` keeps its name. Stories `tree/delete-*`; tests `TestTreeDelete*`. Consumers: matomeru's outline gets `promote` on the next tag (`orphan` has nothing to do there: a task stays under its project's row). `Tree.OrphanParent func(TreeNode) string` names the level orphans go to (default the top level); a failed or refused change (`OnChange` error, or one wrapping `ErrTreeRefused`, which is dropped, not retried) now stops a delete plan: the delete is not written or queued, the rows go back to what was written, and `u` undoes only that. `TreeChange.Undone` marks the changes `u` and `ctrl+r` write. Tests `TestTreeDeletePlanStopsAtAFailedMove`, `TestTreeDeleteOrphanParent`.
- Added: outline `y y`, `p` and `P`. New verbs on `ochazuke.outline` (editable trees; guard `tree-editable`): `yank {register}` copies the row with its subtree into a register as whole lines, a tab per level (so the text pastes from the system clipboard, another outline or an editor), and `put {before, register}` puts a register's rows after the cursor row's subtree as its next sibling (`p`) or before the row (`P`), each row with a fresh id through the add path, nested as yanked, as one undo step; on an empty tree they become the top level, in a filtered view the rows go into the tree and the filter and cursor stay (a status line says when the filter hides them), and an empty register says so. The yank goes through the shared registers, so `"a y y`, the yank history and the clipboard setting work as for any yank. `d d` now fills the register too (a delete: the numbered registers, the clipboard only for `clipboard=unnamed`), what it deleted as indented lines (an expanded row alone, a collapsed one with its subtree, a visual selection's rows), so `d d` then `p` moves a row. In a visual selection `y` copies the selected rows in order (a folded selected row brings its hidden rows) through the same text (`List.yankRows`, set by the tree's list; `list.yank` calls it), so `V j j y` then `p` repeats them; before, the glyph prefix of each row was in the copy. `Y` is unchanged. Bindings `y y`, `p`, `P` in `ochazuke.outline/normal`; unbind them in `bindings.json` to free the keys. Stories `tree/yank-put*`, `tree/delete-put-moves`, `tree/put-*`; tests `TestTreeYankAndPut` and friends. Consumers: matomeru's editable outline gets the keys on the next tag; its pasted rows are new tasks (a title only), as `paste` already makes. `d d` then `p` is a true move: the rows of the delete just made, put back as its own text, keep their ids and structure (once; `y y` then `p` and a second `p` copy). `TreeNode.PutInto` (`putInto`) makes a put at the row (`p`, `P`, paste) go under it as its first children, opened; a put with rows in the tree but none shown says so and adds nothing; an empty register is an info notice; `d d`/`y y` with no row say `no row selected`. Stories `tree/put-into`, `tree/put-nothing-shown`.

## v0.86.0 (2026-11-04)

- Fixed: `List.OnFilter` now hears prop-filter and saved-filter changes on tree lists too (it was text-only on trees), and sees the new saved-filter name; its doc says it follows the whole filter.
- Added: `Parameter.SourceFixed` (catalog `sourceFixed`): constant arguments for a choices source. The derived `restore` verb uses it to offer the archived rows as its target choices, since `list.hide-archived` keeps them out of the default list (a `task restore t1` form no longer saw `t1` and read it as a prefix of `t10`).
- Added: `RowNoun(noun)` instance option, and a list with no rows now keeps the nouns of its last rows (it fell back to `ochazuke.list`), so an empty list (every row archived) keeps its noun's prop schema and saved filters such as `archived`.
- Fixed: a choices-source parameter with only `SourceFixed` and no source (a noun with no list) no longer fails `CheckSourceArgs`; `sourceFixed` is documented in the config export's actions example.
- Changed: a `never` refusal on a noun that has an `archive` verb now adds "or run NOUN.archive" (not on the archive verb itself); destructive verbs keep "agents never delete; run NOUN.archive".
- Fixed: typing a path into a path chooser now just works. A query written as a path (`/`, `~`, `./`, `../`) is answered by the typed source at once from any source (browse, recent, an application's), with no ctrl+o; enter picks the highlighted completion or the typed path itself, and a plain word still filters the list. A folder chooser's enter on the typed folder picks it (the folders under it open); a missing path shows why. The chooser says "type a path" when it has no rows.
- Fixed: enter in a path chooser acts on the query as typed (while a live source loads it, enter waits without blocking and runs when the rows land; esc or typing more drops it; never an older query's row, never `chooser.open` on one); a folder pick offers `new` for a missing path whatever its parent and with a trailing `/`; a chooser's `Hint` (`ChooserSpec.Hint`, the path chooser's "type a path to pick one") ends every empty, no-match and refusal line.
- Added: `ChooserSource.Claims(query)` (a source that recognises the query answers for it; clearing the query, or a hand switch, ends the claim) and the setting `chooser.claim-source` (default on; off leaves the source switch to tab).

## v0.85.0 (2026-11-04)

- Added: a binding row's `present: false` (`Binding.Present`, `Binding.Unpresented()`, published in catalog binding rows) keeps that key's declared text answer out of the viewer; the answer is dropped, not sent to messages. The commands page's `i` row sets it, since the detail pane shows the help.
- Fixed: a synchronous key or palette answer from a verb that moved the focus or page itself (page.open) no longer replaces that verb's own notice with "answer not opened"; only late answers are reported.
- Added: `NounBuilder.Live()` and catalog noun `live`: a noun whose rows are fetched on every call, outside the state verb. A hosted data page leaves its lists out of the state answer (and its delta hashes) and fills them through the noun's row source when the page loads or refreshes, so a live list no longer shows `no rows`.
- Added: `Runtime.PresentResult` shares declared text, Markdown and preformatted presentation between the palette and key bindings. `keys.text-results` defaults to `true`; `false` preserves unpresented key answers. Late key answers do not cover a changed page, focus, mode or modal, and existing completion callbacks run first. CLI/API execution remains non-presenting.
- Added: `Operation.Foreground`, `NounBuilder.Foreground()` and catalog verb `foreground`, preserved by `cli.MountNouns`; `cli.RunMounted` / `ProxyCommand` give hosts catalog-driven captured or real-terminal execution. Only captured runs take a timeout.
- Fixed: hosts strip remote guards only from catalog-published rows. User and host rows retain guard validation. The inspector retains the last nonempty message after the footer clears.
- Fixed: `palette.text-results=all` broadens only palette answers. Key bindings present only declared text formats, so ordinary string or structured key answers do not open text modals; `plain` still disables both paths.
- Added: `messages.show` on `g <` reads the last 32 messages in full. Cut footer messages name its effective key unless `messages.hint=false`. `text-view.wrap` defaults to true; false keeps horizontal scrolling with a clipping marker.
- Fixed: pending palette and key answers share the focus, mode, page and modal check. A result requested inside help opens over help; late answers leave their full text in the message history.
- Added: `TextAnswer{Title, Text}` carries result titles across process boundaries; `text-view.result-title=false` keeps operation-name titles. `Result.OnPresented` lets a host clear its fallback status only after presentation succeeds.
- Added: catalog `slot`, `timeout` and `disabled` metadata. Slots prevent duplicate work per subject row; `jobs.cancel` cancels a running job. Unix captured processes stop as a group and have a one-second record-finalization grace period.
- Added: foreground stderr tails survive return to the host. `foreground.hold=on-failure` waits for a key after nonzero exit; `off` disables the wait. Exit zero never waits.
- Changed: agents can read and write config, bindings, actions, themes and unprotected settings. Destructive verbs default to never, ahead of declarations; owner rights rows still win. An effective allow passes prompts live and headless. Confirmation accept/set remain owner-only.
- Changed: archive is not destructive; settings reset-all and saved-filter deletion are. Reloads and configuration reads carry fetch history kinds.
- Added: `Setting.Protected` keeps file, session and undo writers owner-only for marked settings, even with the rights gate off. The rights rows, gate and confirm attribution are protected; bulk operations skip and report them.
- Added: `list.hide-archived`, default true, hides archive-role records in standard list results; explicit archive conditions override it. Each archive-role noun declares an `archived` saved filter. `get`, `set`, archive and restore keep working by key.
- Added: the About page is always registered once the model starts (`Runtime.EnsureAboutPage`, called by `Model.Init`; `about.open` calls it too) and stays registered after `about.close` or `q`, so the Pages picker (`'`) lists it. It is declared hidden by default (`Page.DefaultHidden`), so it is not a tab and `ctrl+j`/`ctrl+k` skip it; `page.show __about` (pages.hidden) brings the tab back. Rows are still evaluated only when the page opens or refreshes. Story `page-picker/about-from-pick`. Consumers: every app picks it up on the next tag; page lists (`page.list`, the Pages picker) gain an `__about` row. The Settings-page tab (`about.tab`) is unchanged.

## v0.84.0 (2026-11-04)

- Added: stable multi-key list view sorting by props or fallback columns, sibling sorting for trees, shared chooser sorting, `list.sort`, `list.sort-column`, `list.sort-clear` and `z s` cycling ascending / descending / off. `list.sort-indicator` controls arrows; `NoSort()` / `ChooserSpec.NoSort` exclude partial source-owned views. Sort survives row and column replacements, keeps selection by ID and leaves the app order unchanged.
- Added: the filter form defaults to compact field/value pickers with live conditions. `list.filter-layout=fields` retains the field-per-prop form; `list.filter-sort=false` hides sort controls. `list.filter-picker-sort`, default false, separately offers the shared sort-field picker in the older prop chooser. Tab follows sort, direction, fields, values, conditions and save; Shift+Tab reverses it. See [List view sorting](docs/LIST-SORT.md).

- Added: saved filters include optional sort keys in the existing `list.filters` setting. Applying or cycling a legacy definition without sort restores app order. The filter modal now saves/replaces by name, shows the applied name and supports deletion through existing verbs; `list.filter-save` (default on) controls its save row.

- Fixed: filter forms retain absent values, reset removed negations, preserve secondary sort keys through direction changes and undo, and keep exact integers in retained conditions. Saved conditions still apply to `NoSort` views; saving refuses unavailable sort fields. Anonymous chooser rows no longer share a filter/sort cache entry.
- Added: `list.filter-clear-hidden-sort` (default false) preserves a hidden sort on clear unless enabled; `list.sort-sections` (`within` default, `off` to disable local sort on sectioned lists) keeps section headings and source section order intact. Composed record fields can declare `KeepOnClear`.
- Fixed: reopening a filter form retargets the existing form. Pickers retain only a faded unfocused selection, keep the compact modal's height steady and show off-screen matches with `chooser.hidden-count`, default true. Escape returns from values without an edit; Enter keeps a selected value instead of removing it. Normal picker motions use `j`, `k`, `g g` and `G`.
- Fixed: normal-mode editing keys in form text inputs edit that text, including the save name. `u` and `ctrl+r` walk one chain from any stop in a record form: a text input's own history first, then the record's; past the end they do nothing, and `u u ctrl+r` round-trips. `C` and `D` answer from any stop and the palette's `form clear` and `filter-form delete-saved` agree with them. A record form's modal keeps one width through its stops and modes (it no longer follows the footer hints), and the filter form's sort row keeps its width when the direction reverses. `D` opens a saved-filter deletion picker and asks for confirmation. Cycling saved filters is one record undo step. Tab hints come from bindings, conditions retain their search line and the form keeps `ochazuke.form` on its ancestry for user bindings.
- API: `SortKey`, `List.Sort` / `SetSort` / `Sortable`, `Chooser.Sort` / `SetSort`, `NoSort`, `ChooserSpec.NoSort`, `Runtime.AddChooser`, `ChooserSpec.Stay` / `NormalMode`, `RowViewProvider`, `RecordSpec.State` / `Render` / `RowView` / `Immediate` / `OnClear`, and `RecordField.Hidden` / `KeepOnClear` support the composed row-view editor. `List.SetColumns` now rebuilds row caches and reapplies the view sort. The added control nouns are `ochazuke.filter-form`, `filter-save`, `filter-values`, `filter-direction` and `filter-condition`; the editor remains an `ochazuke.form`.
- Added `palette.active-page`, default `true`, to prefer the active catalog page's extension verbs and nouns within existing completion groups. Page switches update the preference; ordinary pages and `false` keep the previous order. Exact commands, aliases, focus priority, guards and execution are unchanged.
- Fixed: a text input and its record form share one undo chain, so `u` and redo step through typing and form changes together. `form.clear` is one undo step and keeps the current mode instead of dropping to normal. `esc` in a filter form's text fields leaves the field without discarding the edit.

## v0.83.1 (2026-11-02)

- Fixed: `ActorFromEnv` detects agent harnesses from their environment markers (declared harnesses, Codex, Claude Code, Pi). A CLI caller with no `OCHAZUKE_ACTOR` was treated as the owner, so agents ran verbs declared `agent: never` (settings, config, bindings, actions, themes) in every app that did not set its own actor. Detection only narrows rights: an explicit owner or unknown actor cannot demote a detected agent. Rights are guard rails, not authentication.
- Changed: agents are now refused those verbs on the CLI, and owner commands run from inside an agent session count as the agent.
## v0.83.0 (2026-11-01)

- Fixed: the story and examples modules use monogatari v0.4.1, whose SGR parsing correctly recognizes faint reversed selections without changing ochazuke's ANSI output. Refreshed six golden highlight gutters and made the timeline border story identify the neighboring list's remembered selection explicitly.

- Fixed: runtime-wide last-find state lives in a registered `search-state` component under `app`, preserving searches across viewers without component fields on `Runtime`. The find line declares `CloseWith(find-line.cancel)` and its insert-mode Escape runs the shared `modal.close`; `find-line.cancel` remains callable. Corrected `modal.pop` alias timing to deprecation in v0.83.0 and removal after v0.84.0.

- Added: `/` finds in text-like components (UX-RULES R5.20, plan row K4's text viewers). The text view, the docs viewer, the Markdown viewer and timelines bind `/` to the new `ochazuke.search.find {query, back, count}`, which opens the find line (noun `ochazuke.find-line`: `enter` `find-line.commit`, `esc` `modal.close` through `CloseWith(find-line.cancel)`, `tab`/`shift+tab` `search.cycle-match`) and moves the cursor to the next match, wrapping; `n`/`N` run the new `ochazuke.search.next {reverse, count}`. The query follows `search.case` and starts in `search.match`'s mode. New interface `TextFinder` (`FindText`), implemented by `Input`, the docs viewer, `markdown.Doc` and `Timeline`; new `Matcher.Spans`, `FindLine` and `Runtime.FindText`/`LastFind`. New setting `search.open-folds` (default on): a find that lands on a closed timeline card by its body opens it. On a timeline and in the Markdown viewer `/` used to reach the list filter through the app row; `ochazuke.list.filter-open` stays, reachable from the palette or a row in `bindings.json`.

- Added: `modal.close`, one close verb for every modal. `esc` and `q` on `ochazuke.modal/normal` run it; it runs the top modal's close request when it has one (`CloseWith(req)` on `OpenModal`, `Runtime.SetModalClose(id, req)` after `Push`, `Runtime.CloseModal`) and otherwise pops it. The chooser closes through `chooser.cancel`, the palette through `palette.cancel` (esc still clears a typed line first), the path picker through `picker.close`, a modal form through `form.leave`. Their `esc` rows (chooser, palette, picker, setting prompt, confirm, where the modal noun is not reached) now run `modal.close`; the per-kind `q` and `esc` rows on the help, docs viewer, text view, Markdown viewer, yank history, settings review and form option list are gone (the modal noun answers), and a placed form's `q`/`esc` rows are guarded `no-modal`. `q` now also closes a confirm without running it. The per-kind verbs stay. Fixes K1 and K2 of the UI consistency assessment. Stories `modals/esc-closes-every-kind`, `modals/q-closes-every-kind`, `modals/pop-restores-focus-and-mode` (R4.3), rule R4.10.
- Renamed: `modal.pop` is now `modal.close`; `modal.pop` is deprecated in v0.83.0 with removal after v0.84.0 (a one-time notice names the new name). Rows and code that run `modal.pop` keep working; an app whose close was a plain pop changes nothing else.
- Changed: macros record on `Q` (start and stop) instead of `q`, so `q` closes modals and viewers in ochazuke's defaults as it does in mtm (owner decision 2026-11-01: one meaning per key across the family). `Q:` opens the command line on the last command (was `q:`). For Vim's `q`, bind it in `bindings.json`: `{"noun": "app", "mode": "normal", "keys": "q", "request": {"noun": "ochazuke.mode", "verb": "enter", "args": {"mode": "macro_pending"}}}` (and the same in `visual`, plus `macro_recording` `q` → `macro.stop` with `when: shadow-idle`).

- Added: shared motions for every component with a cursor or a scroll (UX-RULES R5.8, plan row K6). New interface `CursorMotions` (`MotionEdges`, `MoveToEdge`, `PageLines`, `MovePage`) and verbs `ochazuke.view.edge {edge, row}` (the four edges: `first`, `last`, `first-col`, `last-col`) and `ochazuke.view.page {delta, half, steps}`, guarded `view-edge-moves` / `view-pages`. Implemented by `List` (column edges on a cell-mode table), `Timeline`, `Detail`, the docs viewer and the yank history. Default rows: `g g`, `G`, `ctrl+d`, `ctrl+u`, `ctrl+f`, `ctrl+b`, `0` (when no count) and `$` on `app/normal` (`bindings/scroll.json`), and the same six motion rows on `ochazuke.yank-history`, `ochazuke.docs-viewer/normal` and `ochazuke.timeline-body/normal`. The yank history now answers `g g`/`G`/counts and the page keys; inside a timeline card `g g`/`G` go to the first/last card as on the cards, and the page keys scroll the window.
- Changed: the docs viewer's `g g`/`G` run `view.edge` instead of `detail.scroll` by ±1000000 lines, and its `ctrl+d`/`ctrl+u` run `view.page {half}` (half its window) instead of 15 lines. `detail.scroll`, `list.goto`, `list.scroll`, `timeline.goto` and `yank-history.move` stay.

- Empty, loading and no-match texts follow one format (UX-RULES R5.19): lower case, no parentheses, the rows' noun named. New `Placeholder{Plural, Empty, Loading, LoadingJob}` with `List.SetPlaceholder` (`SetMatchNoun` now sets its `Plural`), and `ChooserSpec.Loading`, `LoadingJob` and `Plural` (a `ChooserSource`'s `Loading` and `LoadingJob` stand in for them while it is current): a list or chooser draws `Loading` while the job in its `LoadingJob` slot (`JobSlot{Scope, Slot}`, as `Result.ReplacingAt`) runs and `Empty` after, so "loading" never reads as "empty". Helpers `EmptyText`, `LoadingText`, `NoMatchText`. Texts changed: `(empty list)` is `no rows`; `(no matches)` and `0 of N (filtered)` are `no rows match 'q'` (with `, 0 of N` when the strip is off); the filter strip's and a chooser's `no matches` name the rows; the picker's `(no matching paths)`/`No selection`/`Loading preview…` are `no paths match 'q'` (`loading paths…` while searching)/`no path selected`/`loading preview…`; a setting's `(empty)`/`(none)` are `empty`/`no items`; a form's `(no values)` is `no values`; `No items.` and `No yanks yet.` are `no items` and `no yanks; …`. Catalog pages read their list's placeholder. Story `lists/empty-format`.

- Added: one selection painter, `Styles.Selection(focused)` (with `Runtime.Selection`, `SelectionStyle` for code with no styles, and `Runtime.StylesFor(a)` for a component drawn in its own appearance). Every selection in the components is drawn through it: lists and tables (their bar path, `selectionBar`, unchanged), the chooser, the path picker, the markdown Doc, the Settings section selector, the grid, record chips, the diff view, the filter's cursor block and the terminal overlay fallback; no component calls `Reverse` itself (UI consistency V1). Without the focus a selection stays marked, faded by `dim-unfocused` plus `dim-unfocused-selection` (mono and `NO_COLOR`: the faint reverse bar), per UX R5.1.
- Fixed: the narrow Settings page's section selector line and a grid's cursor cell no longer drop their highlight without the focus; they keep it, faded (R5.1). Stories `settings/selector-unfocused`, `settings/selector-unfocused-hide`.
- Changed: the path picker draws no `> ` cursor marker; its selected row is the bar alone and every row starts at the left edge (R5.1, R7.1; UI consistency V2).
- Added: setting `unfocused-selection` (appearance, `keep`/`hide`, **default `keep`**): `hide` draws only the focused component's selection (lists, tables, the grid, the Doc, the settings selector, app rows painted with `Styles.Selection`). Opt-out for the change above; `List.UnfocusedSelection` still lets a host hide one list's.

## v0.82.1 (2026-11-01)

- Fixed: visual selection and row/cell yank quietly leave a valid empty list, table or tree unchanged, including registers and clipboard work. Missing list targets still fail, and `List.StartVisual` retains its error contract.

- Added CLI regression coverage: an exact registered noun or noun alias wins over a prefix of another noun alias, including `doc list` beside the framework `docs` alias.

## v0.82.0 (2026-11-01)

- Fixed: editing a long Markdown document no longer pegs the CPU. Glamour draws the document in runs of top-level blocks, each cached, so an edit redraws only the run it touched (output byte-identical); `Tasks` and `Elements` no longer copy the source per item. 10 edits on a 3,000-line doc: 3.0 s → 0.8 s CPU.
- Added `markdown.wrap`, default `true`: the Markdown block editor soft-wraps a long line at the last space that fits as you type (a word wider than the pane breaks at the edge), a list item's next rows under its text; the source gets no line breaks. `false` keeps the sideways scroll.
- Added `Input.Wrap` and `Input.WrapIndent` (soft wrap in `InputBlock`, off by default) and `InputRows(in, width)`.

## v0.81.1 (2026-11-01)

- Fixed: no row field is dropped from prop filters for its value type. An object field (such as a task's `created-by`), a list of non-strings, or a field mixing objects with strings is now a derived prop that compares as its text (compact JSON with sorted keys), the same way mixed kinds already did; its value picker offers those texts. The explicit opt-outs are unchanged: `Secret`, `NoFilter`, a noun's `PropsOff` and the `list.props-off` setting.

## v0.81.0 (2026-11-01)

- Fixed `:h bindings` to open focus-effective key help, matching `:help bindings` and `:bindings help`, so filtering and Escape use the existing help modal and restore its originating focus and mode. Bare aliases can declare an explicit `shadowVerb`, shared by the palette and CLI, without changing alias collision policy or rest-parameter choice arguments.

- Added `markdown.extra-space`, default `false`, to insert one extra blank line between rendered Markdown components, including adjacent checklist items. Existing gaps gain one line; tables, note boxes and component contents retain their internal spacing. The setting is shared by runtime Markdown documents and viewers and does not change source text.
- Added the optional `List.UnfocusedSelection` rendering predicate for hosts whose page lists share a detail view. Nil preserves remembered highlights; false hides only an unfocused page list's selected-row bar. Focused cursors, modal-list behavior, selection state, and existing fade settings are unchanged.
- Added: generic recent-directory navigation in path chooser `recentSource` for file picks (`Opens: true`, `NoPick: true`, reusing `browseSource` directory listing and stepping back out to recent paths); directory picks retain direct selection. Setting `path.recent-dirs` (default `true`) toggles recent directory rows during file picks.

- added: `ochazuke ochazuke.list previous-class`, params `--class`, `--id`, `--steps`

## Historical release notes (formerly Unreleased, as of 2026-10-29)

- Added: rows that stop matching an active text or prop filter remain selectable for `list.filter-linger` (default `30s`; `0` disables it), marked by `~` and the `list.lingering` tone. Query or prop-filter changes flush them immediately; `list.flush-lingering` flushes them while keeping the active filter, including filters using declared prop aliases.

- Added: `PropSpec.Aliases` (`aliases` in catalogs). Alternate prop names resolve to the main field in prop requests, standard reads/writes, `where`, and live/saved prop filters. Filter forms, choosers and prop listings offer the main prop once even when rows retain an alias key. Saved filters resolve aliases on load and use main names when saved again. No new verbs or settings; declarations opt in.

- Added: help and docs text in a modal render through the Markdown component (owner request: "we need to render help docs in modal using md component"). Setting `help.markdown` (section help, default true; false shows them as plain text as before). New result kinds `ResultMarkdown` (`NounBuilder.MarkdownResult`) and `ResultPreformatted` (`NounBuilder.PreformattedResult`, a `--help` page: each paragraph fenced by `markdown.Preformatted`, drawn as written); the palette opens them in the Markdown viewer (modal `markdown-view`, noun `ochazuke.markdown-view`: a read-only `markdown.Doc`, its keys plus `q` close and `y a` copy all, verb `ochazuke.markdown-view.copy`), and `docs open` (`:help`, `:h`) opens its pages there. One mechanism: `ochazuke.RegisterTextViewer(format, viewer)` (package markdown registers Markdown and preformatted text when linked in), `Runtime.TextViewer`, `Runtime.OpenText(TextDoc)`, `IsTextResult`; a ModalBody may implement `AppearanceModalBody` to draw in the model's appearance. `ochazuke.command.help` is now preformatted and `ochazuke.agent.instructions` Markdown; the CLI, API and MCP still return the raw text. A read-only Markdown document now leaves out its writing verbs (guard `markdown-writable`): `i`, `o`, `x`, `d d`, `p`, `n`, `u` do nothing there instead of failing with "this markdown is read-only" (direct calls still refuse). Stories `palette/markdown-result`, `palette/preformatted-result`, `palette/markdown-result-off`, `docs/markdown-viewer`, `commands/help-palette-preformatted`; the earlier docs-viewer stories run with `help.markdown` off. Consumers: hosts linking `ochazuke/markdown` (every `cli` user) get it on the next tag; no code change.
- Added: setting `dim-unfocused-selection` (appearance, integer percent, **default 30**; owner request: "can we dim the focused list item in pane list that is not focused pane"). A page list without the focus draws its selected row's bar faded that many percent further toward the background than the list itself (`dim-unfocused`), so the focused list's bar is the one at full strength: at the defaults the bar sits halfway between the text and background colours, still reverse video, the row readable on it. One rule in the shared paint path (`Styles.selectionBar`, `paintLine`, `List.bar`, `highlightRow`) for lists, tables and trees; a cell-mode table's current cell (`list.cell.cursor`), a run kept out of the bar (`tones.selected-background keep`) and an intersect cell (`list.intersect`) on that row fade with the bar. `0` restores the bar as before (faded with the list alone); mono and `NO_COLOR` keep the faint reverse bar (their only dim). Choosers, the palette and modal lists are unchanged: a modal's list is never drawn unfocused. `Styles.WithSelectionDim(percent)`; `DefaultDimUnfocusedSelection`, `DimUnfocusedSelectionSetting`. Stories `lists/unfocused-bar-dark`, `lists/unfocused-bar-off`.

- Added: the Settings page's All section (setting `settings.all-section`, ochazuke › Settings, default true; false is the page as before). The section list holds an All row first in every tab (an All tab with `settings.layout` `tabs`): every setting in one list, each under a heading naming its tab and section (`ochazuke › Appearance`), edited in place (row ids `__settings/all/row/NAME`; a change and `u` keep the focus in the All list). A `/` search in the section list lands on it, so every matching setting from every section of every tab shows at once (owner request: "filters should filter on every single setting"); `j`/`k` still step to one section's matches. New verb `settings.all` (`A` on the Settings page; from the palette it opens the page). The All row matches by the settings that match one by one, never by the union of every setting. The page still opens on its first real section. Fixed: the section list's panel width counted the widest title and the widest count from different rows apart, so a three-digit count cut a title. Stories `settings/search-all-path`, `search-all-edit`, `show-all`, `show-all-off`; `search-path` and `search-enum-value` now run with the All section off (the opt-out), as do the layout stories whose step counts it would shift; goldens `page-80`, `page-120`, `session-marker-80`, `layout-sections-100`, `filter` gain the All row.
- Added: the prop filter form (owner request: "for filters, like prop filters, … select multiple ones, and also invert it … and that should also be able to be a list"). `z /` in lists, tables and trees and `ctrl+f` in choosers (`ochazuke.list.filter-prop` with no prop) open a record editor over the row view with a field per filterable prop: finite fields show their values as chips (`space` toggles; one is `=`, several `in`, a list prop's a `contains` each), fields with more values than `list.filter-chip-max` (default 24) are text inputs completing the values (`ctrl+n`/`ctrl+p`), the rest text inputs (`contains` on text, `=` otherwise). `!` (`form.invert`) turns a field over; `C` (`form.clear`) empties the form; changes apply live and `esc` keeps them. Setting `list.filter-form` (default true; false keeps the prop chooser then value chooser). Stories `lists/filter-form-*`, `tree/filter-form`, `choosers/filter-form`.
- Added: negated prop filter operators `not-in` and `not-contains` beside `!=` (`FilterOps`, `NegateFilterOp`, `PropFilter.Negate`, `PropFilter.Label`): a negation is the operator's test turned over, one table, no per-op code path. `ochazuke.list.filter-prop` takes `not: true`. Saved filters store the op as written; older rows load unchanged.
- Added: record editor fields `RecordField.Chips` (several choices, a JSON array value, drawn as chips), `RecordField.Invertible` (an is/not state carried as `name!` in the record), `RecordField.Completions`, and `RecordSpec.Clearable`; verbs `form.invert`, `form.clear`, `form.chip-toggle`, `form.chip-move`, `form.chip-line` (spills to the next field at the first and last line) and `form.chip-edge` (`first`, `last`, `first-col`, `last-col`). Field names may not contain `!`.
- Added: `CompletionTrigger.Every`: a trigger with an empty `Trigger` that completes every token (bounded by whitespace and its `Boundaries`). An empty `Trigger` without `Every` is still refused.
- Changed: the module path is `ofam.dev/ochazuke` (was `github.com/unixsuperhero/ochazuke`); the repository moved to `github.com/ofamtools/ochazuke`. Consumers change their imports and `go get ofam.dev/ochazuke@v0.76.0`. The default of `about.dep-prefixes` is now `["ofam.dev/", "github.com/ofamtools/", "github.com/unixsuperhero/"]`.
- Fixed: the key resolver judged a binding row's availability at the focus while `Execute` judged it where the row runs (its target, or the level it matched at when the operation is that level's own), so a row the target refuses could win the key and then be refused. Since the `list.no-spill` edges (a motion at a trapped edge is unavailable), `j` in a timeline card's body at the last card of a timeline trapped at `:last` fell through to the list's `list.move`, which won at the body and showed "list.move is not applicable now" instead of doing nothing. The resolver and the live listings (hints, `?`, bindings list) now ask `Available` at the row's instance (`Runtime.rowInstance`, shared with `runRow`): such a row stands aside and the key goes on (to the next row, or a silent no-op), at all four edges (`:first`, `:last`, `:first-col`, `:last-col`). Behaviour change: a level's own row reached from a non-row child (a card body under its list) whose verb guard reads the focused list now judges that list, so it can answer there where it used to stand aside (a table's `list.column` row bound on the table, from a child). Tests `TestTrapResolveJudgesRowAtItsTarget`, `TestTrapResolveHintsJudgeAtTarget`; story `timeline/no-spill-body-silent`.
- Added: `Runtime.TypingShadows()` (`typing_check.go`): while a field takes text (an input in insert mode or a mode extending it, a list's filter line, a chooser's or the palette's query, a form field, an outline's open line: any focus and mode where a level of the walk answers the `printable` class), every printable character and space must reach that field. The check resolves each key the way `Dispatch` does, runs nothing, and reports the row that answers first (a capture row, a row nearer the focus, a mode-less row) or the sequence that would make the key wait. Permanent checks: `TestNoPrintableKeysWhileTyping` (the framework's typing places), `TestMarkdownTypingIsNotShadowed`, and `TestStoryTypingIsNotShadowed` in `cmd/o-story` (every story's runtime at its start and after each event). An application calls it on its own runtime the same way.
- Changed: the Markdown editor's block chooser (`ochazuke.markdown.insert-block`, on an empty line in insert mode) moved from `/` to `alt+/`; `/` is typed like any other character. Bind `/` back in `bindings.json` (`ochazuke.markdown-edit/insert`, `when: markdown-empty-line`) to restore the old key. Stories `markdown/block-chooser`, `markdown/block-chooser-table` press `alt+/`; new stories `lists/filter-types-z-q`, `inputs/insert-types-z-q`.
- Changed: a cell-mode table's current cell draws on a background instead of an underline (o-json owner request: "on right side, change style of focused cell (bg not underline)"). New tone `list.cell.cursor` (`ToneCellCursor`; every built-in theme: the scheme's surface on its accent, bold), drawn as reverse video with the colours swapped so the cell stays part of the selection's run. New setting `list.cell-cursor` (`CellCursorSetting`, section appearance): `background` (default) or `underline` (the old look, reversed and underlined inside the bar). mono and `NO_COLOR` have no background and keep the underline, so mono goldens are unchanged. The header still underlines the current column's title. Tests `TestCellCursorBackground`, `TestSelectedRowDrawsToneAttributesOnly` (both modes); stories `tables/cell-cursor-background`, `tables/cell-cursor-underline`; the Settings page's Appearance section counts 13 (stories `settings/tab-step`, `search-path`, `layout-sections`, golden `layout-sections-100`). Needs monogatari's `cellText` to read reverse-on-a-background as the current cell (lane `mono-cell-bg`) for a story's `cell` expect. Consumers: o-json, o-csv and every cell-mode table pick it up on the next tag with no code change.

- Changed: the `?` help's `/` filter finds a row by its key in every way the key is shown or typed, in every view (here, pick, table): the key cell as drawn (`z h`, `ctrl+d`), the raw key (a pattern row's `<digit>`) and each sequence without its spaces (`zh`, `gx`, `ctrl+ao`); the table view also matches each mode cell's full operation (`demo.reset` finds `.reset`). The extra spellings ride on the rows' hidden search text, no matcher change. New `TreeNode.Search` (as `ListItem.Search`: text the tree's `/` filter matches a node by, never drawn). Tests `TestTreeFilterSearchText`, `TestHelpKeySearchSpellings`, `TestHelpFilterFindsKeys`; story `help/filter-by-key`.
- Fixed: an app's top-level oden command wins over a mounted namespace of the same word, as it wins over a framework short name. A namespace that is no noun (an extension's `mcp` of `mcp.item`, the framework's `ochazuke`) is now the CLI group `NS.` (`cli.NamespaceSuffix`) with `NS` as an alias; before, the namespace group took the word whenever `cli.Mount` ran first, so with an extension registered as `mcp` (o-mcp) `mtm mcp serve` failed with `subcommand "serve" not found` and both harnesses' MCP registrations broke. The namespace's nouns stay reachable as `NS.NOUN` (read as `NS. NOUN`) and by their bare aliases; help prints `shadows alias mcp of mcp. namespace` under the owner. Help lists a namespace as `NS., NS`. Test `TestMountAppCommandOwnsANamespaceWord`. Consumers: nothing to change.
- Added: `sysstat` segment `proc`, this program's own CPU use (getrusage `RUSAGE_SELF` user+system time over wall time between samples, macOS and Linux, `ErrUnsupported` elsewhere; same 2s cadence and off-loop refresh as `cpu`), labelled with the program's name (`mtm 118%`, else `proc`) and drawn beside `cpu`. Setting `status.proc-scale` (Status bar): `core` (default, one core = 100%, as Activity Monitor and top) or `machine` (normalised over every core, as `cpu`); hot at 90% of the machine in both. On/off as the others: `Options.Enabled` and `status.segments.proc`. New `sysstat.Proc`/`NewProc`, `IDProc`, `ProcScaleSetting`, `ScaleCore`, `ScaleMachine`. Segment rows: a provider's or verb's string argument may name a setting as `"{name}"`, read on the loop at each refresh (as binding rows do). Tests `TestProc*`, `TestRegisterProc`, `TestSegmentArgsNameASetting`; story `segments/proc-beside-cpu`. Consumers: matomeru (already calls `sysstat.Register` with `Enabled: true`) shows it on the next tag with no code change.
- Added: settings `help.operation-max` (default 32) and `help.description-max` (default 80), section help. The `?` help's operation column grows to its longest operation up to the cap (at least 18 cells when narrow) instead of a fixed 18 that wrapped `outline-task.toggle`; the arguments column and the table view's mode columns take the same cap (were 22 and 24); the description wraps within its cap and the room left stays empty, so a wide terminal no longer stretches it. 0 in both restores the earlier layout. `helpColumns` is now built from the settings (`Runtime.helpColumns`). Goldens changed (the operation column fits its widest cell, a cell or more moving to the description or arguments): `testdata/help-80.golden`, stories `help/leader-sequences`, `help/pick-place`, `help/pick-view`, `help/prefix-columns-80`, `help/prefix-columns-120`, `help/sequence-steps`. New stories `help/columns-80`, `help/columns-100`, `help/columns-200`, `help/columns-off-200`.
- Added: the Settings page's `/` searches every setting (setting `settings.search-values`, ochazuke › Settings, default true; false is the title-only section filter as before). A section row matches by its settings' names, descriptions, values, defaults, saved values, enum choices and list items; while the section list filters it spans every tab under owner headings, and the settings pane shows the shown section's matching settings; the settings list's own `/` matches the same fields. An empty result names what was searched (`no settings match 'path'`); esc restores the page on the row it opened on. New list API: `ListItem.Search` (filter text that is never drawn), `List.OnFilter` (hear filter changes), `List.SetMatchNoun` (the empty line's wording). Stories `settings/search-path`, `search-enum-value`, `search-off`.
- Added: the walk path source (`path_walk.go`). `Runtime.AddWalkPathSource(func(PathPick) PathWalk)` gives every path chooser a `walk` source (`PathSourceWalk`) over the roots an application names (`PathWalk{Roots []PathRoot{Path, Label}, Batch}`; `Runtime.WalkSource(pick, walk)` builds one to add under another name). It lists every folder under the roots (files for a file pick), breadth first: the roots show at once (no file system read), then the folders under them, walked off the loop in batches that stream into the chooser, so the shallow folders show first and the list stays responsive; closing the chooser or loading again cancels it. Rows show the path relative to its root and the root's label; the filter reads the whole path. A root inside one listed before it is listed once; a finished root walk is reused for 30 seconds. New settings (ochazuke › Choosers): `path.walk` (default on; off leaves the source out, as before), `path.walk-depth` (8), `path.walk-limit` (20000; the title says when it stops there), `path.walk-ignore` (`.git`, `node_modules`, `vendor`, `.cache`, `target`, `__pycache__`, `.venv`), `path.walk-hidden` (off), `path.walk-include` (`.worktrees`: hidden names walked anyway, since a repo's worktrees are folders worth picking), and `path.first` (`["walk"]`: the sources listed first in every path chooser, and the one it opens on when none is named or remembered; empty keeps the old order). The walk never enters a folder under `path.no-auto-list`.
- Added: streaming chooser loads. A source's load may return `ChooserBatch{Rows, More}`: the rows show at once and `More` (pending work) loads the next part, appended as it lands; the chooser stays loading until a part has no `More`.
- Changed: a space in a fuzzy query also matches a separator (`/`, `-`, `_`, `.`), so `mato cmd` finds `~/proj/matomeru/cmd/mtm` (choosers, the path picker and the fuzzy list filter).
- Tests `TestPathWalk*`, `TestFuzzySpaceMatchesSeparator`; stories `forms/path-walk-finds-deep`, `forms/path-walk-streams`, `forms/path-walk-depth`, `forms/path-walk-off` (the story page field `pathWalk`).
- Changed: on an outline's open line (insert mode) `tab` and `shift+tab` run what they do on the row in normal mode, the row noun's own rows first, then go on editing the line with the cursor (or select range) where it was. New verb `ochazuke.outline.row-key {key}`; the `ochazuke.outline-line/insert` rows for `tab`/`shift+tab` now name it (hints `indent`, `outdent`); binding them back to `ochazuke.outline.indent`/`outdent` restores the old behaviour. An application that binds `tab` on a row to its own verb (mtm's project-to-task conversion) now gets it from the line too, instead of a raw move under the row above. Stories `tree/insert-tab-runs-row-key`, `tree/insert-tab-indents-keeps-cursor`; docs/reference regenerated.
- Added: the About page (`about.go`). `ochazuke.about.open` opens a framework page of sections: Build (module, version, commit, commit time, modified, Go version, binary, from `debug.ReadBuildInfo` with the `refdoc` linker stamps over it), the application's sections (`Runtime.AddAboutSection(title, func() []AboutRow)`, evaluated when the page opens or refreshes, never at startup; `AboutRow{Label, Value, Kind, Note}`, `Kind: AboutPath` for a file or directory), Dependencies (modules under the prefixes of the new setting `about.dep-prefixes`, default `["github.com/unixsuperhero/"]`, a local replace or a go.work build said) and Runtime (pid, start, uptime, the status segments' last values). Verbs, all agent-allowed and read-only: `about.open`, `about.close`, `about.refresh`, `about.show {section}` (data for the CLI and MCP), `about.copy {section}` (the report as text, yanked); rows are the `about-row` noun with `show`, `copy` and `open` (a path row, through `AboutEnv.Open`, default `open`/`xdg-open`). Keys (`bindings/about.json`): on the page `Y` `about.copy`, `r` `about.refresh`, `q`/`esc` `about.close`, `]`/`[` (and hidden `g t`/`g T`) `settings.tab` (guard `about-tab`); on a row `y` `about-row.copy`, `enter` (hidden `o`) `about-row.open` (guard `about-row-path`). No key opens the page. `Runtime.SetAboutEnv(AboutEnv{...})` fixes the build and process for tests and stories (o-story page spec `about`; stories `about/page`, `about/tab-off`).
- Added: `Runtime.LinkSettingsTab(SettingsTabLink{Title, Page, Open, Close, Setting})`, a Settings page tab that is a page of its own, drawn after the owners' tabs while its boolean setting is on; `settings.tab` steps onto it (running Open) and back from its page, closing the page left; `Runtime.SettingsTabTitles(page)` is the tab row a linked page draws. The About page is the first link, behind the new setting `about.tab` (default on; off is the opt-out). The Settings page's tab row now ends with About (goldens `settings/page-80`, `page-120`, `filter`, `session-marker-80` updated; `settings/tab-step` steps through it), and the ochazuke tab gains an About section holding the two settings.
- Added: every field of a list's rows is a filterable prop unless turned off; only declared fields are writable. A noun that declares no props (an extension's catalog rows, o-lane's `lane`) now filters on its rows' fields with `z /`, `z !`, `z C` and saved filters (`list.filters`, `NounBuilder.Filters`, catalog `filters` over an undeclared field). Derived props are read from the rows lazily, cached until the rows change, typed from the values (string, number, boolean, list of strings with `contains`; mixed kinds compare as text; objects skipped), and are `ReadOnly`/`Derived`: never written by `set`, forms, palette, CLI or MCP (`set` says "only declared props are writable"). A declared `PropSpec` still wins. Off only explicitly: `Secret` props; `PropSpec.NoFilter`; `NounBuilder.PropsOff` / catalog `nouns[].propsOff`; the new setting `list.props-off` (list of `NOUN.FIELD` patterns, default empty; `["*"]` turns prop filters off). A table row without data gets a field per titled column. The value picker caps at 500 values. The `z /` footer hint is now hidden (still in `?`), so it does not crowd every list's footer; goldens: Settings page Search count 9 to 10, and the settings footers lose `z / prop filter`. Story `lists/props-from-rows`; tests `TestRowProps*`, `TestPropFilterCatalogRowsOfferFields`.

- Fixed: a terminal whose process exits without changing its screen (cat on EOF where the pty does not echo ^D, as on Linux) showed "running" until the next key under frame coalescing. `TerminalFrame` gains `Status` (the stream's own state, e.g. `running`, `exited 0`); a read whose Status changed always redraws, drawn or hidden, and `cli.PtyStream` fills it. Tests `TestRenderTerminalStatusChangeRedraws`, `TestRenderBackgroundCompletionShowsInNextFrame`, `TestCommandsShowAnExitThatLeavesTheScreenAlone`, `TestPtyStreamFrameCarriesTheExit`.
- Added: focus stops for inline Markdown links in paragraphs, headings, list and task items, quotes, notes and table cells. `markdown.link-stops` defaults to `true`; `false` restores element-only movement. `j`/`k` and counts visit links in drawing order, while component and kind jumps remain element-wise. The focused link uses the theme accent within its element's selection and stays visible across reflow. `enter`/`g x` open it through an injectable platform opener, `y l` copies its URL without changing `y y`, and `] u`/`[ u` jump between links. The four actions are palette/CLI reachable and mirrored in captured mode. Edit mode keeps source text behavior.

- Added: render caching and frame coalescing. Background messages (job ticks, terminal output, settings polls) no longer rebuild every frame: the model keeps its last `tea.View` with dirty/urgent flags and composes at most `render.max-fps` frames per second (setting, default 30, 0-240; 0 turns frame reuse and coalescing off). Input, paste, resize and API calls still draw at once, and the last frame of a burst is always drawn. Styles are cached per appearance/theme/tone generation; binding walks, help rows and footer hints are memoized and invalidated by binding, focus, mode, guard and alias changes; viewports reuse wrapped text by content generation and size; chrome text widths use an ASCII fast path and a bounded cache; child indexes are cached by registry generation. `Model.InvalidateRender()` forces the next frame for hosts. Measured (benchmarks): idle poll + View 462 us to 1 us, busy terminal update 405 us to 23 us, styles 24x, full frame 2.5x. One golden changed: the Settings page Appearance section count, 11 to 12, for the new setting. Story `terminal/render-cap-off`. Patterns adapted from tuios (MIT, Gaurav Gosain).

- Added: reuse of unchanged surfaces: shell and modal composition and fitted cells are cached by input, hidden terminals are not rendered, and ticker allocations are reduced, so an unchanged or hidden feed costs 0 compositions per second (was 10). Explicit test snapshots bypass the frame clock. Consumers: matomeru caches its pane panels and columns on top (mtm release with this tag); measured on the owner's mtm: idle CPU from ~30-42% to 0.5-8%.

- Added: setting `list.no-spill` (ochazuke › Lists, list of instance ids, default empty): a list, tree or timeline it names keeps its `j`/`k` motions (and a table's `h`/`l` at the column edge, a timeline's last step) inside it at the edges, as `NoSpill()` does, instead of moving the focus to the next or previous stop. A tree or timeline is named by its own id (`panes/working`); an entry ending in `*` is a prefix (`panes/*`). Read on every motion, so a change applies at once; a `NoSpill()` list stays trapped whatever the setting says. Every spill path asks one helper (`List.trapped`). Story `lists/no-spill-setting`; tests `TestListNoSpillSetting*`, `TestTreeNoSpillSetting`. Consumers: matomeru (Panes page Working tree: `"list.no-spill": ["panes/working"]`), o-json and every app with lists on a page pick it up on the next tag; no code change needed.

- Added: `list.no-spill` entries name an edge: `ID:last` (past the last row: `j`, `down`, a count from the edge, a timeline's last step), `ID:first` (before the first row), `ID:last-col` / `ID:first-col` (a table's, or a tree with columns', `l`/`h` at its column edge); several entries combine (`["t:last", "t:last-col"]`), no suffix traps all four as before, and `NoSpill()` still traps all four. One edge set (`SpillEdge`, `SpillEdges`, `ParseNoSpillEntry` in `spill_edges.go`) serves every list-like component; one without horizontal motion (a plain list, a timeline) takes the column edges as a no-op. An unknown suffix stays part of the id (names nothing). At a trapped edge the motion is now unavailable instead of a silent no-op: `list.move` has the guard `list-moves`, `list.column` and `list.scroll-columns` by a delta the added availability `list-column-moves`, `timeline.step` the added `timeline-step-moves`; the hints and `?` help leave the key out there (the help's table view now marks `.move?`) and the key goes on to the next row bound to it. A clamped `list.move {clamp}` stays available; paging never spilled and is unchanged. `markdown.Doc.MoveCursor` leaves a trapped line move as a no-op instead of an error. Story `lists/no-spill-edge`, `tables/no-spill-edge`; tests `TestParseNoSpillEntry`, `TestSpillEdgesMatrix` (4 edges × list, table, tree, timeline × no setting, all, each edge, a prefix), `TestSpillEdgeMotionUnavailableInHelp`, `TestSpillEdgeKeyFallsThrough`, `TestSpillEdgeTableRowsCountsAndPaging`, `TestSpillEdgeTimelineStep`. Consumers: matomeru's Working pane list: `"list.no-spill": ["panes/working:last"]` (stops at its last row, still spills up at its first); every app picks it up on the next tag.

- Added: catalog-generated MCP startup instructions with an embedded family preamble, app/noun guidance, actor/undo and rights explanations, quoted and capped extension text, and deterministic text budgets (1,200-token target, 1,800 hard cap including advertised tool descriptions). `ochazuke.agent.instructions` and `mcp instructions` share initialize/resource/guide output. `cli.MountCatalog` preserves mounted app descriptions and rules; oversized host declarations fail catalog construction. No new setting.

- Fixed: app top-level oden commands and runtime/catalog nouns win over framework short names. Oden commands may be added before or after `cli.Mount`; o-json's add-commands-first workaround is no longer needed. This fixes the v0.71.0 `mcp` regression. Framework verbs remain available under full names (`BIN ochazuke mcp serve`, `ochazuke.mcp.tools`); help and mounted catalogs identify active shortcuts and app commands. MCP config uses the full server command.

- Fixed: agent-rights preferences use the normal embedded startup declaration and validation path, preserving application-first setting order and config export/redeclaration. Dry-run validation is an execution argument; bound transport identity and inherited owner approval remain documented runtime request-chain state.
- Fixed: the command palette uses `Model.ModalViews` like other modals, so a caller's `ModalViews["palette"]` renderer and title take precedence. Default palette rendering is unchanged; covered palettes use the shared cursor suppression.

- Added: headless catalog MCP server in `cli/mcp`, with four tools (`catalog`, `describe`, `run`, `state`), strict tool argument validation, protocol negotiation, result-data notes, resource/guide mirrors and data-driven legacy tool aliases. Mounted extension verbs are hidden from discovery by default (`mcp.include-extensions=false`). Every CLI shell gains `mcp serve|tools|instructions|config`; runs share CLI checks, actor history, jobs and exact undo. `run.dry_run` checks without running. Added a temp-data task example and o-mcp client e2e. Owner approval now follows a composite action's nested requests; headless composite confirmations refuse without opening a modal.

- Added: agent rights gate on every agent request, ordered owner overrides in `agents.rights`, default-denied config/settings verbs, owner-only confirmations and optional confirmation attribution/status segment. CLI and socket transports bind startup actors and ignore request actor overrides; force and Confirmed cannot bypass policy.

- Added: `Runtime.SetDefaultBindings(source, rows)` atomically replaces a source's default rows and reloads the user binding layer above them. Overrides and unbinds survive; removed defaults are reported without discarding user rows, and later reloads cannot restore stale defaults. Pending sequences, generated cancels, hints and help follow the replacement. No new setting.

- Added: extension catalog `sides` declarations (`cli.CatalogSide`, `DeclareSide`, `CatalogDoc.ValidateSides`) and argv-only `ExpandSideCommand` for `{dir}`, `{pane}`, `{session}`, `{project}`, `{shell}` and `{bin}`. Hosts own launching and lifecycle; declarations add no process, binding, page or setting. `catalog inspect` lists the templates in each tool's details. Schema 1 retains the optional section. Inspector faint text now has readable contrast in light and dark themes.

- Fixed: `ctrl+p` from no highlight selects the last row in choosers, source/path choosers and inline completion, shared with palette completion. `chooser.up-from-none` defaults to `last`; `stay` restores no selection on up. Existing bindings, verbs and edge policies remain. Owner decision 2026-10-31 supersedes UX review 2 F1.

- Fixed: catalog detail sections have outer whitespace and indented thread bodies separated from their author/time header. `detail.padding` defaults to `1` (one blank line, two inset cells); `0` restores the tight layout, and catalog pages may override `padding`.

- Added: Markdown completed-task visibility. `markdown.done` defaults to `show`; `hide` removes checked tasks and their full subtrees from rendering and cursor motions without changing the source. `markdown.done-show` / `done-hide` / `done-toggle` set document-local session visibility (palette/CLI, no default key). A dim pinned `N done hidden` header is enabled by default; `markdown.done-indicator=false` opts out. Checking a visible task advances to the next visible element, falling back at the end. Stories `markdown/done-*`.

- Added: request actors (owner by default; explicit agent identity through API or CLI environment), `Ctx.Actor()`, history attribution and `history.list {actor, limit}`. Confirmations and pending callbacks retain identity. `history.undo {entry}` targets a session entry; agents undo only their own, with checks against overwriting later edits. Existing history files and owner requests remain compatible.

- Added: catalog guidance for agents: app/noun descriptions, agent rules, named guards, examples, destructive flags and declared/default agent rights, preserved through extension mounting and shown in catalog inspect. Standard delete and archive declare destructive intent.

- Added: data-driven catalog detail sections (`markdown`, `thread`, `fields`), read-only in every host without a renderer registration. Main Markdown wraps completely; supporting sections draw on the right or below, with `detail.side` (default true), `detail.side-width` (96), per-page overrides and `detail.sections` (true) to restore the legacy record. Metadata has labelled formats and full-value copy/expand through `g m`, then `y`/`enter`. Fields-only catalogs keep their rendering. Added `markdown.WrapAll()` for display wrapping without clipping long code or words.

- Added: application-defined list row classes (`ListItem.Classes`, `NounBuilder.Classes`, `ClassDecl`) and `list.next-class` / `list.previous-class` motions. Declarations generate overridable `] KEY` / `[ KEY` binding rows with counts, help and hints. Motions visit shown rows, clamp without spilling, and report plain status when no match remains. Empty keys are palette-only; undeclared lists are unchanged. Stories `lists/class-motions`, `lists/class-help`.

- Added: optional `diffview` package for structured or unified-patch file diffs, unified/split/auto layouts (140-column default), lazy Chroma highlighting, theme-derived added/removed tints and changed-word marks. Counted hunk/file rows, shared line scrolling, horizontal scrolling, binary/truncated summaries, MIT tuios attribution, targeted goldens and `diff/*` stories. Hosts own layout/highlight/context settings and file loading.

- Added: optional tree branch lines with `Guides()` (`guides` in story specs and catalog tree lists), the dim `tree.guide` tone, and `tree.guides=app|off` (default `app`, trees opt in). Folding, zoom and text/prop filters retain correct branches. Catalog lists can opt into trees (`tree`, row `parent`), preserving ordinary list defaults. Stories `tree/guides`, `tree/guides-filtered`, `tree/guides-folded`, `tree/guides-off`.

- Fixed: prop-filter focus jumps reveal hidden rows; tree filtering preserves deep selection, marks and row instances, and `/` alone keeps its previous fold behavior. Prop-filter context can be folded with h/l/enter and clearing restores original folds. Saved-filter cycling skips invalid definitions with a message; malformed `list.filters` JSON is refused on write and reported on load. Zero matches remain visible with the strip off. Row props/search text, row nouns and parsed saved settings are cached. Added `list.filter-save`, `filter-saved-delete`, `filters` and `filter-saved-pick` to manage user definitions. The footer shows `z /`, with `z !` hidden.

- Added: framework prop filters on lists and trees (`PropFilter`: `=`, `!=`, `contains`, `in`, ANDed with `/` text). Trees keep matching descendants' ancestors, including collapsed paths, and restore collapse state on clear. `z /` picks a prop and observed value, `z !` removes one condition, `z C` clears prop conditions. `NounBuilder.Filters` and catalog `nouns[].filters` declare named defaults; the `list.filters` setting adds user definitions scoped by noun or list. `list.filter-saved` applies a name; `z ]` / `z [` cycle saved filters. `list.filter-strip` and `list.filter-cycle` default on and opt out of the respective UI. Standard noun lists accept `--filter NAME` beside unchanged `--where`, before `--limit`. Tests and stories cover lists, tree ancestors, cycling, clearing, CLI and catalog mounting. Matomeru's mine/agents/all adoption is a separate consumer lane.

- Fixed: the inspector toggle uses `alt+?`, an unused single key, so it no longer turns an editor's `g` into an app-level sequence and steals `g y` or the count of `g g`. The `ochazuke.state.toggle` verb is unchanged; the default stays off. Story `inputs/inspector-prefixes`.
- Added: live state inspector (`Runtime.Inspect`, `Inspection.Compact`/`Parts`, `ochazuke.state inspect` with CLI JSON), an `alt+?` toggle and `inspector.enabled` (default off). Includes modes, focus/row path, bounded register values and yank head, last message/tone, request/result (including job completion), selection, marks, count/prefix, modals, jobs and reported writes. Apps add fields with `AddInspectField` and report writes with `RecordWrittenFile`; config/binding exports report their paths. Story `lists/inspector-yank`.

- Added: events and hooks (lane event-hooks, plan `mtm-tasks-source-of-truth` lane C). `Runtime.Emit(event, fields)` and an event registry (`DeclareEvent`, `Events`, `EventSpec{Name, Description, Fields, Source}`); the framework emits `surface.opened` and `surface.left` when a modal, form or page opens or closes (`kind`, `id`, `noun`; on leave `changes`, `changed` (the nouns changed while it was open, counted where a change verb ran or `Runtime.Changed` was called; the framework's own `ochazuke.*` nouns and a hook's own changes are not counted) and `opened_at`) and `app.quitting` (what changed since the last `surface.left`; the Model waits up to `QuitHookWait` for its hooks' jobs). Rows `{on, where, do, args, from, off}` in `hooks.json` run verbs on events in order, an application verb as a job off the loop; a failing hook is a notice. The app owns its hooks config: each application reads its own `<config dir>/hooks.json`, merged after its own defaults (`AddDefaultHooks`) and its extensions' catalog defaults (`CatalogDoc.Hooks`/`Events`, `cli.MountCatalogHooks`); there is no framework or global hooks file, and extensions have none. Verbs `hooks list|check|export|reload|path` (`export` defaults to `<config dir>/reference/hooks.reference.json`) and `events list|emit`; `config reload` reads hooks.json. Setting `hooks.enabled` (default on) turns every hook off. README "Events and hooks" has the comparison with Vim's autocommands. Stories `events/*`.
- Changed: `bindings export` no longer requires a path (owner: "path should not be required...should default to .config/matomeru/references/"). With none it writes `<config dir>/reference/bindings.reference.json`, the file `config export` writes (same `Config.Paths().Reference` directory, kept singular `reference`), creates the directory and returns the path it wrote; with no config directory it asks for a path. `--path` works as before and never writes over the live `bindings.json` without `--force`; an explicit path's directory is created too.
- Fixed: `h` on a tree row with nothing to do (a closed or childless top-level node outside a zoom) no longer swallows the key: `ochazuke.outline.collapse` is unavailable there (guard `outline-collapses`), and the tree's list takes the table's `h` rows (R5.11), so on a tree with columns `h` scrolls the columns back and then moves the focus to the previous stop, as the flat table did (found by matomeru's Work task tree). Setting `tree.spill` (ochazuke › Lists): `left` (default), `both` (`l` on a leaf also falls through to the next stop) or `off` (a tree's list never takes the table's `h`/`l` rows). `l` on a leaf still stays in the tree by default (R11.1). Stories `tree/h-spills-left`, `tree/h-spill-off`, `tree/l-spill-both`; test `TestTreeCollapseUnavailableWithNothingToDo`. Consumers: matomeru (Work tree, Panes trees, outline modal), o-json (structure tree) pick it up on the next tag.
- Added: `ChooserRow.Tones []ToneSpan`: a chooser row's runs drawn in theme tones, as a list row's are (`Cell` indexes `Cells`, or 0 for the `Label`); the highlighted row keeps its reverse bar.
